Cacheweft bounded closeout

Passed: True

Official installed systemd service in a disposable Linux VM. Fixed 180 seconds / 90 cycles; same PID, epoch and invocation, no restart during soak. RSS/FD/threads are daemon-only, Linux VM measurements; metadata growth from retained request history is expected. A bounded observation is not proof of no leaks or long-term rollover. No power-loss or autostart qualification.
